\frac{d}{dt}\left(e^{\phi(t,D_{x})}V(t,x,y)\right)=-\dot{\theta}(t)\left|D_{x}\right|e^{\phi(t,D_{x})}V(t,x,y)+e^{\phi(t,D_{x})}\partial_{t}V(t,x,y),

where
-\dot{\theta}(t)\left|D_{x}\right|e^{\phi(t,D_{x})}u(t,x,y)
gives a smoothing effect if
\dot{\theta}(t)\geq 0
. This smoothing effect allows to obtain our global existence and stability results in the analytic framework.

Proof of global well-posedness of system (
1.9
)

By applying the dyadic operator in the horizontal variable
\Delta_{q}^{h}
to (
3.6
) and taking the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation with
\Delta_{q}^{h}(V_{\phi})
we obtain

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A(D)V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

(3.7)

In what follows, we shall use the technical lemmas in Section
2
, to handle term by term in Estimate (
3.7
).

By applying the result of Lemma
2.3
, we find that

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\Big(\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big),

(3.8)

and by using the fact that
(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}=\partial_{t}u_{\phi}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)|D_{x}|u_{\phi}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A(D)V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(3.9)

We sum the two equality (
3
) and (
3
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A(D)V_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(3.10)

Now, for the pressure term, using the Dirichlet boundary condition
(u,v)|_{y=0}=(u,v)|_{y=1}=0
, and the incompressibility condition
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
, by performing an integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle D=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}\partial_{y}v)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=0,

In the last step we use the fact that
\partial_{y}p_{\phi}=0
. Thus,

\displaystyle D=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=0.

While due to
u/_{y=0}=u/_{y=1}=0
, by applying Poincaré inequality, we have

\displaystyle k\|\Delta^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.11)

where
k
is the Poincaré constant.
Then, multiplying (
3
) by
e^{2\mathcal{R}t}
(
\mathcal{R}
is a constant smaller than Poincaré constant denoted by
k
), we achieve

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
\leq\mathcal{R}\left(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\\
+\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|.

(3.12)

In what follows, we shall always assume that
t<T^{\star}
, where
T^{\star}
given by

T^{\star}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny def}}{{=}}\sup\left\{t>0,\ \ \ \theta(t)\leq\frac{a}{\lambda}\right\}.

(3.13)

Next, we note that

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} &J^{q}_{1}(t)=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\\
&J^{q}_{2}(t)=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|.\end{aligned}\right.

To estimate those two non linear terms we need to use Lemmas
2.5
and
2.6
. In view of Lemma
2.5
, we replace
C_{\phi}
by
(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}
and
A=B=u
, then we conclude the following estimate of
J^{q}_{1}
for any
t<T^{\star}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J^{q}_{1}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})},

(3.14)

where
\dot{\theta}(t)=\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.

In view of Lemma
2.6
, we replace
C_{\phi}
by
(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}
,
A=u
and
B=v
, then we conclude the following estimate of
J^{q}_{2}
for any
t<T^{\star}

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J^{q}_{2}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})},

(3.15)

where
\dot{\theta}(t)=\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
.

Then we deduce from
(
3.12
)
by integrating with respect to the time interval, that

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
\leq\mathcal{R}\left(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

(3.16)

We multiply the estimate (
3.16
) by
2
, we obtain

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+2\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
\leq 2\mathcal{R}\left(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right)+2C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

(3.17)

Now we still have to get some information of the norm
\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, for that we need to apply the dyadic operator
\Delta_{q}^{h}
to the equation

e^{\phi(t,|D_{x}|)}(\partial_{t}^{2}u+\partial_{t}u+u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u-\partial_{y}^{2}u+\partial_{x}p)=0,

(3.18)

and then, we take the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation (
3.18
) with
\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}
, we obtain

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

(3.19)

In what follows, we shall use again the technical lemmas in Section
2
, to handle term by term in the estimate (
3.19
). We start by the term

I_{1}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\text{ \ and \ }I_{2}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

so by using integration by parts, we find

\displaystyle I_{1}=\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}

\displaystyle\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dX-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}dX

\displaystyle+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dX,

and

\displaystyle I_{2}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Whereas due to the boundary condition, and by integrating by part, we achieve

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(-\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}),\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Now, by using the Dirichlet boundary condition
(u,v)|_{y=0}=(u,v)|_{y=1}=0
, and the incompressibility condition
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
and the relation
\partial_{y}p_{\phi}=0
, we can find by integrating by parts the estimate of the pressure

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}v_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=0.

Then by using Lemma
2.1
and by multiplying (
3.19
) by
e^{2\mathcal{R}t}
, we achieve

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int

\displaystyle e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dX-\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}dX+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dX

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=2\mathcal{R}\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dx+\mathcal{R}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

(3.20)

Next, we note that

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} &L^{q}_{1}(t)=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\\
&L^{q}_{2}(t)=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\\
&L^{3}_{q}(t)=\left|2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dX\right|.\end{aligned}\right.

In view, of lemma
2.5
-
2.6
, we can deduce for
t<T^{\star}
that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}L^{q}_{1}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}

(3.21)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}L^{q}_{2}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}.

(3.22)

Then we still have to estimate
L^{3}_{q}
, therefore by cutting the derivative
|D_{x}|
into two half derivative, we achieve the

\displaystyle L^{3}_{q}(t)

\displaystyle=\left|2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}dX\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int\left|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right|\left|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\right|dX

\displaystyle\leq\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+{\lambda}\dot{\theta}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(3.23)

Next, by taking the integration over time of (
3
), and we sum it with
(
3.17
)
, we gather that

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+\int_{0}^{t}2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\leq\mathcal{R}\int_{0}^{t}\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]dt^{\prime}+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+{\lambda}\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+2Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\Big).

(3.24)

We begin with by observing that the term in the square brackets of the right-hand side in (
3.24
) can be absorbed by the dissipation
\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}
. Indeed, since the value of
\mathcal{R}
is smaller than
\min\{\frac{1}{8},\frac{k}{8}\}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}

\displaystyle\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{R}\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{k}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

To absorb this last term, we shall then invoke the Poincaré inequality in
y\in(0,1)
:
k\|\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}
. Thus

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}

\displaystyle\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

(3.25)

We replace the obtained result (
3
) in (
3.24
), we deduce that

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}\\
+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
\leq 2Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\Big).

(3.26)

In the last estimate (
3.26
), we remark that the term
\int_{0}^{t}\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}
has disappeared because of the second term that comes out of the estimate (
3
). We can make it appear by using Poincaré inequality on the term
\int_{0}^{t}2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle 2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda\Big(k\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big).

Now we we multiply the obtained result in (
3.24
) by
2^{2qs}
for
s\in]0,1[
and summing with respect to
q\in\mathbb{Z}
, we find that for
t<T^{\star}

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)\\
+\frac{1}{2}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\sqrt{k\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\Big)\\
+\sqrt{2}C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{2}C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

(3.27)

Taking
\lambda\geq 4C^{2}
and
k\lambda\geq 8C^{2}
in the above inequality leads to

\mathcal{E}_{s,\frac{\lambda}{4}}(u)(t)\leq C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}},\text{ \ for \ }t<T^{\star}.

(3.28)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s,\frac{\lambda}{4}}(u)(t)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{k\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

We recall that we already defined
\dot{\theta}(t)=\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
with
\theta(0)=0
. Then, for any
0<t<T^{\star}
, Inequality (
3.28
) yields

\displaystyle\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\times\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\right\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle<\frac{a}{2\lambda}

We deduce from the continuity argument that
T^{\star}=+\infty
and we have (
3.28
) is valid for any
t\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
.

4.
Propagation of the regularity and of the vorticity of the hyperbolic Prandtl equation (
1.9
)

In this section, we present first a proposition states the propagation for any
\mathcal{B}^{s}
regularity on the solution of the perturbed hyperbolic Navier-Stokes equations (
1.9
). The second proposition allows us to control two derivatives in the normal direction
\partial_{y}^{2}
in any
\mathcal{B}^{s}
, despite the difficulties raised by the boundary conditions. Those propositions will be useful in the last section when we prove the global convergence of the Theorem
1.3
. In what follows, we shall always assume that
t<T^{\star}_{a}
, where
T^{\star}_{a}
given by

T^{\star}_{a}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny def}}{{=}}\sup\left\{t>0,\ \ \ \theta(t)\leq\frac{a}{\lambda}\right\}.

(4.1)

Proposition 4.1
.

We assume that the condition (
1.11
) is satisfied, then for any
s>0
, there exist a small constant
C
, such that for

\lambda=C(1+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}),

we have

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)\\
+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
\leq C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}},\text{ \ for \ }t<T^{\star}_{a}.

(4.2)

Proof of Proposition
4.1
. We first deduce from Lemma
2.5
that for any
s>0
we have by replacing
A=B=u
and
C=\partial_{t}u

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(T^{h}_{u}\partial_{x}u+R^{h}(u,\partial_{x}u))_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

(4.3)

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

Then we still have to proof only that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(T_{\partial_{x}u}^{h}u)_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})},\text{ \ \ for \ \ }s>0.

Indeed, in view of (
2.2
), we infer

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(T_{\partial_{x}u}^{h}u)_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\int_{0}^{t}e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

which leads to

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

(4.4)

While it follows from the proof of Lemma
2.6
that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}u}v+R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}u))_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})},

so we have yet to determine the estimate of
\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}
, we have

\|\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\phi}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim d_{q}(u_{\phi})2^{\frac{q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

so that

\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

which implies that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

By summing all the terms we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\right).

(4.5)

Along the same way we can obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}.

(4.6)

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\right).

(4.7)

By virtue of
(
4.4
)
,
(
4
)

(\ref {esti:111bis1})
and
(\ref {esti:222bis1})
, we deduce from
(\ref {S3eq2bis1})
and (
3
) that for
t<T^{\star}_{a}

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
+\sqrt{k\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\Big)\\
+\sqrt{2}C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{2}C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+\sqrt{2}C\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\sqrt{2}C\|(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.8)

Applying Young’s inequality yields

\displaystyle C\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|

\displaystyle e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{10}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}.

Then we achieve

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
+\sqrt{k\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+c\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
\leq C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
+C(2+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u,\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

(4.9)

Therefore if we take

\displaystyle\lambda\geq 4C^{2}(2+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})})

\displaystyle k\lambda\geq 8C^{2}(2+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}),

(4.10)

we obtain

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s,\frac{\lambda}{4}}(u)(t)\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\Big),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s,\frac{\lambda}{4}}(u)(t)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+c\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{k\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

which in particular implies that under the condition (
1.11
), there hold

\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\leq\sqrt{C}\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big).

Then by taking
\lambda=C(2+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}})
, therefore the condition of the proposition is satisfied and then the proposition is proved for any
t>0
.

Proposition 4.2
.

We assume that the condition (
1.11
) is satisfied, then for any
s>0
, there exist a small constant
C
, such that for

\lambda=C(1+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}),

we have

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)\\
+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\leq C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+C\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+2}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+1}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+1}},\text{ \ for \ }t<T^{\star}.

(4.11)

Proof.

of Proposition
4.2
.
We start our proof by applying the partial derivative on
y
(
\partial_{y}
) to (
1.9
), we obtain

\partial_{t}^{2}\partial_{y}u+\partial_{t}\partial_{y}u+\partial_{y}(u\partial_{x}u)+\partial_{y}(v\partial_{y}u)-\partial_{y}^{3}u+\partial_{x}\partial_{y}p=0.

Due to the free divergence condition of
U
(it mean that
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
), we have

\displaystyle\partial_{y}(u\partial_{x}u)+\partial_{y}(v\partial_{y}u)

\displaystyle=\partial_{y}u\partial_{x}u+u\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u+\partial_{y}v\partial_{y}u+v\partial_{y}^{2}u

\displaystyle=\partial_{y}u\partial_{x}u+u\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u-\partial_{x}u\partial_{y}u+v\partial_{y}^{2}u

\displaystyle=u\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u+v\partial_{y}^{2}u,

so our equation becomes

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}w+\partial_{t}w+u\partial_{x}w+v\partial_{y}w-\partial_{y}^{2}w+\partial_{x}q=0

(4.12)

where
w=\partial_{y}u
and
q=\partial_{y}p
. from which, using that
-\partial_{y}w+\partial_{x}p
is vanishing on the boundary, we get, by using a similar derivation of (
3.12
) and (
3
), it mean that we do the scalar product of our equation with
\Delta_{q}^{h}(w+2\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}
, so that

\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
\leq 2\Big(\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}w)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle\right|_{L^{2}}+\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}w)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle\right|_{L^{2}}\Big)\\
+2\mathcal{R}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+2\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi}\cdot e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0))_{\phi}dx\right|,

(4.13)

and

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\mathcal{A}(w_{\phi})(t)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(4.14)

\displaystyle=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}w)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}w)_{\phi},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\mathcal{R}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}dX\Big)-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi}\cdot e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0))_{\phi}dx

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}(w_{\phi})(t)

\displaystyle=\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}dX-\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}dX

\displaystyle+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}dX

Now, We start to estimate the pressure term, for date we denote

K_{q}=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi}\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0))_{\phi}dx.

In view of (
3.4
) and Lemma
2.3
, we write

\displaystyle K_{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi}\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0))_{\phi}dx

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big((w_{\phi}(t,x,1)-w_{\phi}(t,x,0)\Big)\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}_{\phi}dy)\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big((w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}^{2}dx+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|\Big((w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}^{2}dx

\displaystyle-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}_{\phi}dy)\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}dx

\displaystyle-\mathcal{R}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big((w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}^{2}dx

If we use Lemma
2.3
to the following quantity
\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot e^{2\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}dx
, we can obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(\partial_{t}w(t,x,1)-\partial_{t}w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)dx

\displaystyle-2\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u\partial_{t}u)dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)dx

\displaystyle+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}|D_{x}|\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)dx.

So we multiply our equation by
e^{2\mathcal{R}t}
, we achieve that

\displaystyle|e^{2\mathcal{R}t}K_{q}(t)|\leq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{d}{dt}|\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)dx|

\displaystyle+2\int_{\mathbb{R}}|e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u\partial_{t}u)dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)|dx

\displaystyle+2\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\int_{\mathbb{R}}|e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}|D_{x}|\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)|dx

\displaystyle+2\mathcal{R}|\int_{\mathbb{R}}e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\phi}\partial_{x}(\int_{0}^{1}(u)^{2}dy)\cdot e^{\phi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)dx|

\displaystyle+\mathcal{R}|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\Big((w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0)\Big)_{\phi}^{2}dx|.

from which, we infer

\displaystyle|e^{2\mathcal{R}t}K_{q}(t)|\leq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u^{2})_{\phi}\|_{L^{1}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\Big({\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}+{\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}(u^{2})_{\phi}\|_{L^{1}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+C\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{1}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle+\mathcal{R}\Big(\frac{3}{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u^{2})_{\phi}\|_{L^{1}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}^{2}\Big).

By applying Bony decomposition, we have for any
s>0

\displaystyle\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u^{2})_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{1}_{v}(L^{2}_{h}))}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u^{2})_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}2^{-qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{\infty})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}2^{-qs}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}

and

\displaystyle\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}

\displaystyle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{1}_{v}(L^{2}_{h}))}\lesssim\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}2^{-qs}\Big(\|u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{\infty})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}+\|\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{\infty})}\|e^{\mathbb{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}2^{-qs}\Big(\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}+\|\partial_{t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}\Big).

While notice that

\int_{0}^{1}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t,x,y)dy=0,

then for any fixed
(t,x)\in\mathbb{R}^{+}\times\mathbb{R}
, there exist
Y_{0}^{q}(t,x)
so that
\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t,x,Y_{0}^{q}(t,x))=0.
So we have

(\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t,x,y))^{2}\leq 2\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}},

which implies that

\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t,x,y))\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}(L^{2}_{h})}\leq 2\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}.

As a result, it comes out

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|e^{2\mathcal{R}t}K_{q}(t^{\prime})|dt^{\prime}\leq d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\Big(C\mathtt{E}(0)+C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}

(4.15)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}^{2}+\lambda\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}+C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}^{2}

\displaystyle+C\lambda\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}+\frac{\lambda}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}

\displaystyle\frac{3\mathcal{R}}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}+\frac{3\mathcal{R}}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}+\mathcal{R}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}^{2}\Big)

where

\displaystyle\mathtt{E}(0)=\|e^{a|D_{x}|}w_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}^{2}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}w_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}^{2}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+1}}^{2}

Along the same way we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|e^{2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi}\cdot\Delta_{q}^{h}(w(t,x,1)-w(t,x,0))_{\phi}dx|dt^{\prime}

(4.16)

\displaystyle\leq d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\Big(C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}+C\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{2}\Big).

It follows from the proof of Lemma
2.5
, for any
s>0

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{u}\partial_{x}w+R^{h}(u,\partial_{x}w))_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq C\tilde{d}_{q}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

While we deduce from Lemma
2.1
and Definition
2.3

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T_{\partial_{x}w}^{h}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{x}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\int_{0}^{t}\|w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

Then we conclude for any
s>0

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}w)_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

(4.17)

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

In the same way we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}w)_{\phi},e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

(4.18)

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}.

On the other hand, we deduce from Lemma
2.1
that for any
s>0

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}w)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t(^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

In the same way we obtain that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}w)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

Finally, we use Lemma
2.5
, we find

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}w}v)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}\|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}t(\mathcal{B}^{s)}}.

Then by summing we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}w)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}\|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s)}}

\displaystyle+Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

(4.19)

Along the same way we can found that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}w+R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}w))_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}.

Then we still have to estimate
T^{h}_{\partial_{y}w}v
, so by integration by part we can obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}w}v)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle|dt^{\prime}\leq\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{w}\partial_{y}v)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{w}v)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}.

Due to the free divergence
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
we deduce

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{w}\partial_{y}v)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}.

While we observe that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{w}v)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|w_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}.

By summarizing the above estimates, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}w)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}w_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}|dt^{\prime}\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

(4.20)

\displaystyle+Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-2qs}\Big(\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\Big)

)-(

) in (

) + (

) and multiplying by

for

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(w+\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
+\sqrt{k\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
\leq C\Big(\mathcal{L}(0)+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+\|w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+\|\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+2})}\Big)\\
{\sqrt{\lambda}}\big(C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\big)+C\sqrt{\lambda}\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}

(4.21)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(0)=\mathtt{E}(0)+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}w_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(w_{0}+w_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}w_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}.

Applying Young’s inequality yields

\displaystyle C\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{4}}\|

\displaystyle e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}.

Then we achieve

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(w+\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{3}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
+\sqrt{k\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
\leq\sqrt{C}\Big(\mathcal{L}(0)+\Big(\sqrt{2}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\big(1+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\big)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+\big(\sqrt{2}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{y}w,\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+\|\partial_{t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+1})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{s+2})}\Big).

(4.22)

Therefore if we take

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} \lambda&\geq 8C\Big(2+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\Big)\\
k\lambda&\geq 8C\Big(2+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}),\end{aligned}\right.

(4.23)

we obtain by using the

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s,\frac{\lambda}{4}}(w)(t)

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\mathcal{L}(0)+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+2}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+1}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s+1}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\lambda}\big(1+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\big)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\Big)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s,\frac{\lambda}{4}}(w)(t)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(w+\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{3}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{\sqrt{k\lambda}}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}w)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{\sqrt{\lambda}}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}w_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\theta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

Then, taking
\lambda=8C(2+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}),
therefore the condition of the proposition is satisfied and then the proposition is proved.
∎

As a matter of fact, it remains to present the estimate of
\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}
, this estimate will serve us in the proof of the last theorem
1.3
. Indeed by applying
\Delta_{q}^{h}
to (
3.18
) and take the
L^{2}
inner product of resulting equation with
\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}
. That yields

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

The fact that
(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}=\partial_{t}u_{\phi}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)|D_{x}|u_{\phi}
implies

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\theta}(t)2^{q}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

from which, we deduce that

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq I_{1}+I_{2}+I_{3}+I_{4},

where

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle I_{4}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|.

Since
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
, using (
3.1
) and integrations by parts, we find

\displaystyle I_{4}=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=0.

For
I_{1}
,
I_{2}
and
I_{3}
we have

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\leq C\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{10}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\leq C\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{10}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle I_{3}

\displaystyle=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\leq C\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{10}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Then, we deduce that

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq C\left(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\right).

Multiplying the result by
e^{2\mathcal{R}t}
and integrating over
[0,t]
, we get

\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(L^{2})}^{2}\\
\leq C\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}^{2}\Big).

Multiplying the above inequality by
2^{3q}
, then taking the square root of the resulting estimate, and finally summing up the obtained equations with respect to
q\in\mathbb{Z},
we obtain

\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\Big).

(4.24)

Next, to deal the nonlinear terms in (
4.24
), we need the following lemma that give the estimates of the terms
\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}
and
\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}
, the proof will be given in the appendix
8
.

Lemma 4.1
.

\displaystyle\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})};

(4.25)

and

\displaystyle\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}+\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

(4.26)

Inserting the above estimates into (
4.24
) and then using the smallness condition
\left\|u_{\phi}\right\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\leq\frac{1}{4C^{2}}
and propositions
4.1
and
4.2
, we finally obtain

\displaystyle\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}u_{1}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}(u_{0}+u_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big).

5.
Global well-posedness of system (
1.8
)

The goal of this section is to prove the Theorem and to establish the global well-posedness of the system
(\ref {eq:HPNhydroPE})
with small analytic data. As in Section 2, for any locally bounded function
\Theta
on
\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}
and any
u\in L^{2}(\mathcal{S})
, we define the analyticity in the horizontal variable
x
by means of the following auxiliary function

\displaystyle u_{\Theta}^{\epsilon}(t,x,y)=\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{\xi\rightarrow x}(\epsilon^{\Theta(t,\xi)}\widehat{u}^{\epsilon}(t,\xi,y)).

(5.1)

The width of the analyticity band
\Theta
is defined by

\Theta(t,\xi)=(a-\lambda\tau(t))|\xi|,

where
\lambda>0
with be precised later and
\tau(t)
will be chosen in such a way that
\Theta(t,\xi)>0
, for any
(t,\xi)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}
and
\dot{\Theta}(t)=\Theta^{\prime}(t)=-\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\leq 0
. In our paper, we will choose

\displaystyle\dot{\tau}(t)=\|\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}^{\epsilon}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\epsilon\|\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}^{\epsilon}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{ \ \ \ \ with \ \ \ \ }\tau(0)=0.

(5.2)

In what follows, for the sake of the simplicity, we will neglect the script
\epsilon
and write
(u_{\Theta},v_{\Theta})
instead of
(u_{\Theta}^{\epsilon},v_{\Theta}^{\epsilon})
. In view of the system (
1.8
), we can transform it like a equation of order one in time, so if we define
U=(u,\partial_{t}u)
and
V=(v,\partial_{t}v)
, Then
U
and
V
satisfy the following equation

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}U+A_{\epsilon}(D)U=-\begin{pmatrix}0\\
