u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u+\partial_{x}p\end{pmatrix}\\
&\epsilon^{2}\Big(\partial_{t}V+B_{\epsilon}(D)V\Big)=-\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\epsilon^{2}(u\partial_{x}v+v\partial_{y}v)+\partial_{y}p\end{pmatrix}\\
&\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0\\
&(u,v)/_{y=0}=(u,v)/_{y=1}=0\end{aligned}\right.

(5.3)

where

U=\begin{pmatrix}u\\
\partial_{t}u\end{pmatrix}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }A_{\epsilon}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\
-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}&1\end{pmatrix}

and

V=\begin{pmatrix}v\\
\partial_{t}v\end{pmatrix}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }B_{\epsilon}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\
-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}&1\end{pmatrix}

Then in view of (
2.3
) we observe that
(U,V)_{\Theta}
verifies

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}U_{\Theta}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)|D_{x}|U_{\Theta}+A_{\epsilon}(D)U_{\Theta}=-\begin{pmatrix}0\\
(u\partial_{x}u)_{\Theta}+(v\partial_{y}u)_{\Theta}+\partial_{x}p_{\Theta}\end{pmatrix}\\
&\epsilon^{2}\Big(\partial_{t}V_{\Theta}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)|D_{x}|V_{\Theta}+B_{\epsilon}(D)V_{\Theta}\Big)=-\begin{pmatrix}0\\
\epsilon^{2}(u\partial_{x}v+v\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}+\partial_{y}p_{\Theta}\end{pmatrix}\\
&\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}+\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}=0\\
&(u_{\Theta},v_{\Theta})/_{y=0}=(u_{\Theta},v_{\Theta})/_{y=1}=0\end{aligned}\right.

(5.4)

Where
|D_{x}|
denote the Fourier multiplier of the symbol
|\xi|
. In what follows, we recall that we use ”C” to denote a generic positive constant which can change from line to line.

By applying the dyadic operator in the horizontal variable
\Delta_{q}^{h}
to (
5.4
) and taking the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation with
\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}
and
\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}
we obtain

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A_{\epsilon}(D)U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\phi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

(5.5)

and

\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}B_{\epsilon}(D)V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}v+v\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}p_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

(5.6)

As in (
3
) and by using Lemma
2.3
, we gather

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}u_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\Big(\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|u_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big),

(5.7)

and

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}v_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\Big(\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|v_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

(5.8)

Next, we use that
(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}=\partial_{t}u_{\Theta}+\lambda\dot{\tau}|D_{x}|u_{\Theta}
and
(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}=\partial_{t}v_{\Theta}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)|D_{x}|v_{\Theta}

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A_{\epsilon}(D)U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

and

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}B_{\epsilon}(D)V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\epsilon^{4}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}^{2}v_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}v_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

We use again the result of Lemma
2.3
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A_{\epsilon}(D)U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

(5.9)

and

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}B_{\epsilon}(D)V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

(5.10)

We sum (
5
) with (
5
), we achieve

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A_{\epsilon}(D)U_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}U_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

(5.11)

Next we sum (
5
) with (
5
), we obtain

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}B_{\epsilon}(D)V_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}V_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

(5.12)

By using the Dirichlet boundary condition
(u,v)|_{y=0}=(u,v)|_{y=1}=0
, and the incompressibility condition
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
and the relation, we can perform integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\nabla p_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=0.

We insert the resulting equality (
5
) in (
5.5
) and (
5
) in (
5.6
), and then multiplying by
e^{2\mathcal{R}t}
, we achieve

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\epsilon^{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{4}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
\leq\mathcal{R}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\epsilon^{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\Theta},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle\right|_{L^{2}}\\
+\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\Theta},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\right\rangle\right|_{L^{2}}+\epsilon^{2}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|\\
+\epsilon^{2}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|.

(5.13)

In what follows, we shall always assume that
t<T^{\star}_{1}
, where
T^{\star}_{1}
given by

T^{\star}_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tiny def}}{{=}}\sup\left\{t>0,\ \ \ \tau(t)\leq\frac{a}{\lambda}\right\}.

(5.14)

Then we deduce from Lemmas
2.5
-
2.7
for any
t<T^{\star}_{1}
, and integrating over time (
5.13
), that

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}\\
+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
\leq\mathcal{R}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)\\
+C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\Big).

(5.15)

We multiply the estimate (
5.15
) by
2
, we have

\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}\\
+2\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+2\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+2\epsilon^{2}\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
\leq 2\mathcal{R}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)\\
+2C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\Big).

(5.16)

Now we still have to get some information of the norm
\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
and
\|\partial_{x}u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}
, for that we need to apply the dyadic operator
\Delta_{q}^{h}
to the equation

\displaystyle e^{\Theta(t,|D_{x}|)}\Big(\partial_{t}^{2}u+\partial_{t}u+u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u-\partial_{y}^{2}u-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u+\partial_{x}p\Big)

\displaystyle=0

\displaystyle e^{\Theta(t,|D_{x}|)}\Big(\epsilon^{2}(\partial_{t}^{2}v+\partial_{t}v+u\partial_{x}v+v\partial_{y}v-\partial_{y}^{2}v-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}v)+\partial_{y}p\Big)

\displaystyle=0,

(5.17)

and then, we take the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation (
5
) with
\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}
and
\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}
, we obtain

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}p_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}},

(5.18)

and

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}^{2}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}^{2}v_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}^{2}v_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}v+v\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}p_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}},

(5.19)

In what follows, we shall use again the technical lemmas in Section
2
, to handle term by term in the estimate (
5.18
) and (
5.19
). We start by the complicate term
I_{1}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}
and
I_{2}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}^{2}v)_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}
, so by using integration by parts, we find

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}dX-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}dX

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}dX

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}dX-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}dX

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}dX

Whereas due to the boundary condition, and by integrating by part, we achieve

\displaystyle-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\Theta}+\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u_{\Theta}),\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(-\epsilon\partial_{y}^{2}v_{\Theta}-\epsilon^{3}\partial_{x}^{2}v_{\Theta}),\epsilon\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Now, by using the Dirichlet boundary condition
(u,v)|_{y=0}=(u,v)|_{y=1}=0
, and the incompressibility condition
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
, we can find by integrating by parts the estimate of the pressure

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\nabla p_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}(u,v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}p_{\Theta},\Delta_{q}^{h}\text{ div }(u,v)_{\Theta}\right\rangle\right|=0.

Then by multiplying (
5.18
) and (
5.19
) by
e^{2\mathcal{R}t}
, and integrating the resulting inequality over time, we achieve

\int\bigg(\frac{d}{dt}\Big(e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\Big)-e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}+2\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\bigg)dX\\
+\int\bigg(\frac{d}{dt}\Big(e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\Big)-e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}+2\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\bigg)dX\\
+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t)\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(u_{\Theta},\epsilon v_{\Theta})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}\epsilon v_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
=\mathcal{R}\Big(2\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}dX+2\int e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon v)_{\Theta}dX+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u_{\Theta},\epsilon v_{\Theta})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)\\
-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u+v\partial_{y}u)_{\Theta},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}u_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}v+v\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta},e^{2\mathcal{R}t}\Delta_{q}^{h}v_{\Theta}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

(5.20)

In view, of Lemma
2.5
-
2.6
for
t<T^{\star}_{1}
, and by summing
(
5.16
)
with (
5.20
), we obtain

\mathbf{B}(u,v)(t)\leq\mathcal{R}\int_{0}^{t}\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u+u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v+v)\big)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+2\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]dt^{\prime}+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\\
+\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+2C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\bigg(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
\times\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\Big)+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u_{\Theta},\epsilon v_{\Theta})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\Bigg).

(5.21)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{B}(u,v)(t)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u+u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v+v)\big)_{\Theta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)\big)_{\Theta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\lambda\big(\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle+2\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}

We begin with by observing that the term in the square brackets in (
5.21
) can be absorbed by the dissipation

\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}

. Indeed, since the value of
\mathcal{R}
is smaller than
\min\{\frac{1}{8},\frac{k}{8}\}
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}

\displaystyle\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u+u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v+v)\big)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]\leq\mathcal{R}\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(u,\epsilon v\big)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]\leq\frac{k}{8}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(u,\epsilon v\big)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

To absorb those terms, we shall then invoke the Poincaré inequality in
y\in(0,1)
:
k\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}
. Thus

\mathcal{R}\Big[\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u+u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v+v)\big)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+2\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big]\\
\leq\frac{1}{2}\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big).

(5.22)

We replace the obtained result (
5.22
) in (
5.21
), we deduce that

\mathbf{F}(u,v)(t)\leq 2C2^{-2qs}d_{q}^{2}\bigg(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}u_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\times\Big(\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}\Big)+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u_{\Theta},\epsilon v_{\Theta})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\Bigg),

(5.23)

where

\mathbf{F}(u,v)(t)=\int_{0}^{t}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u+u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v+v)\big)_{\Theta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\big(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)\big)_{\Theta}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\epsilon^{2}}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+k\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\epsilon^{2}\lambda\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{x}u,\epsilon\partial_{x}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)dt^{\prime}.

(5.24)

If we look at the third line of the equality
(
5.24
)
, we remark that we have a new term

k\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime},

this term handle from the Poincaré inequality applied to the following term

\lambda\int_{0}^{t}\dot{\tau}(t^{\prime})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{y}u,\epsilon\partial_{y}v)_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

Multiplying (
5.21
) by
2^{2qs}
for
s\in]0,1[
and then integrating over time, and summing with respect to
q\in\mathbb{Z}
, we find that for
t<T^{\star}_{1}

\mathbf{E}_{s,\lambda,k}(u,v)(t)\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}(u_{0},\epsilon v_{0})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\epsilon\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{x}(u_{0},\epsilon v_{0})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{1},\epsilon v_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1},\epsilon(v_{0}+v_{1}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
+\sqrt{2}C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}((\partial_{t}u)_{\Theta},\epsilon(\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{2}C\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})},

(5.25)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{E}_{s,\lambda,k}(u,v)(t)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u,\epsilon(v+\partial_{t}v))_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}

\displaystyle+\epsilon\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\sqrt{k\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{\epsilon}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}+\sqrt{\lambda}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\tau}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{s+\frac{1}{2}})}

Taking
\lambda\geq 2C^{2}
in the above inequality leads to

\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u+\partial_{t}u,\epsilon(v+\partial_{t}v))_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
+\epsilon\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\Big)+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(\partial_{t}u,\epsilon\partial_{t}v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}+\frac{1}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\\
+\frac{\epsilon}{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{x}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{s})}\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}(u_{0},\epsilon v_{0})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\epsilon\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{x}(u_{0},\epsilon v_{0})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\\
+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{1},\epsilon v_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1},\epsilon(v_{0}+v_{1}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{s}}\Big),\text{ \ for \ }t<T^{\star}_{1}.

(5.26)

We recall that we already defined
\dot{\tau}(t)=\|\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}^{\epsilon}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\epsilon\|\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}^{\epsilon}(t)\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{ with }\tau(0)=0
. Then, for any
0<t<T^{\star}_{1}
, Inequality (
3.28
) yields for
s=\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\tau(t)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\Theta}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\epsilon\|\partial_{y}v_{\Theta}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}e^{-\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{0}^{t}e^{-2\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}(u,\epsilon v)_{\Theta}\right\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}(u_{0},\epsilon v_{0})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\epsilon\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{x}(u_{0},\epsilon v_{0})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{0}+u_{1},\epsilon(v_{0}+v_{1}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\|e^{a|D_{x}|}(u_{1},\epsilon v_{1})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\Big)<\frac{a}{2\lambda}

A continuity argument implies that
T^{\star}_{1}=+\infty
and we have (
3.28
) is valid for any
t\in\mathbb{R}_{+}
.

6.
The convergence to the perturbed hydrostatic Navier-Stokes equations

In this section, we justify the limit from the scaled perturbed anisotropic Navier-Stokes system to the perturbed hydrostatic Navier-Stokes system in a 2D thin domain. As in the sections 3 and 4, the main idea will be to obtain a control of the difference between the two solutions in analytic spaces, by using energy estimates with exponential weights in the Fourier variable. As previously, the exponent of the exponential weight is depending on time but shall take into account now the ”loss of the analyticity” for both solutions, of the re-scaled perturbed Navier-Stokes system and respectively of the perturbed hydrostatic Navier-Stokes equations. To this end, we introduce

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} R^{1,\epsilon}=u^{\epsilon}-u,\\
R^{2,\epsilon}=v^{\epsilon}-v,\\
q^{\epsilon}=p^{\epsilon}-p.\end{aligned}\right.

(6.1)

Then, systems (
1.8
) and (
1.9
) imply that
(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon},q_{\epsilon})
verifies

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}R^{1,\epsilon}+\partial_{t}R^{1,\epsilon}-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}R^{1,\epsilon}-\partial_{y}^{2}R^{1,\epsilon}+\partial_{x}q^{\epsilon}=F^{1,\epsilon}\ \ \mbox{in}\ \mathcal{S}\times]0,\infty[,\\
\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\left(\partial_{t}^{2}R^{2,\epsilon}+\partial_{t}R^{2,\epsilon}-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}R^{2,\epsilon}-\partial_{y}^{2}R^{2,\epsilon}\right)+\partial_{y}q^{\epsilon}=F^{2,\epsilon},\\
\displaystyle\partial_{x}R^{1,\epsilon}+\partial_{y}R^{2,\epsilon}=0\\
\displaystyle\left(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon}\right)|_{t=0}=\left(u_{0}^{\epsilon}-u_{0},v_{0}^{\epsilon}-v_{0}\right),\\
\displaystyle\partial_{t}\left(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon}\right)|_{t=0}=\left(u_{1}^{\epsilon}-u_{1},v_{1}^{\epsilon}-v_{1}\right),\\
\displaystyle\left(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon}\right)|_{y=0}=\left(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon}\right)|_{y=1}=0,\end{array}\right.

(6.2)

where the remaining terms
F^{i,\epsilon}
, with
i=1,2
, are determined by

\displaystyle\left\{\begin{aligned} F^{1,\epsilon}&=\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u-(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}-u\partial_{x}u)-(v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}-v\partial_{y}u),\\
F^{2,\epsilon}&=-\epsilon^{2}\left(\partial_{t}^{2}v+\partial_{t}v-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}v-\partial_{y}^{2}v+u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}v^{\epsilon}+v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}\right).\end{aligned}\right.

(6.3)

As
(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon})
satisfies the boundary condition and also the free divergence, therefore these two conditions allows us to write

\displaystyle R^{2,\epsilon}(t,x,y)

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{y}R^{2,\epsilon}(t,x,s)ds=-\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{x}R^{1,\epsilon}(t,x,s)ds

(6.4)

If we replace
y
by
1
in (
6.4
), we deduce from the incompressibility condition
\partial_{x}R^{1,\epsilon}+\partial_{y}R^{2,\epsilon}=0
that

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\int_{0}^{1}R^{1,\epsilon}(t,x,y)\,dy=-\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{y}R^{2,\epsilon}(t,x,y)\,dy=R^{2,\epsilon}(t,x,1)-R^{2,\epsilon}(t,x,0)=0.

In what follows, for simplicity, we shall neglect the subscript
\epsilon
in
(R^{1,\epsilon},R^{2,\epsilon},q^{\epsilon},)
. In view of the system (
6.2
), we can transform it like a equation of order one in time, so if we define
G=(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})
and
H=(R^{2},\partial_{t}R^{2})
, Then
G
and
H
satisfy the following equation

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}G+A_{\epsilon}(D)G=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
F^{1}-\partial_{x}q\end{pmatrix}\\
&\epsilon^{2}\Big(\partial_{t}H+B_{\epsilon}(D)H\Big)=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
F^{2}-\partial_{y}q\end{pmatrix}\\
&\partial_{x}R^{1}+\partial_{y}R^{2}=0\\
&(R^{1},R^{2})/_{y=0}=(R^{1},R^{2})/_{y=1}=0\end{aligned}\right.

(6.5)

where

G=\begin{pmatrix}R^{1}\\
\partial_{t}R^{1}\end{pmatrix}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }A_{\epsilon}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\
-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}&1\end{pmatrix}

and

H=\begin{pmatrix}R^{2}\\
\partial_{t}R^{2}\end{pmatrix}\text{ \ \ and \ \ }B_{\epsilon}(D)=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\
-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}&1\end{pmatrix}.

In view of (
2.3
), we define for any suitable function
f

\displaystyle f_{\varphi}(t,x,y)=\mathcal{F}^{-1}_{\xi\rightarrow x}\left(e^{\varphi(t,\xi)}\widehat{f}(t,\xi,y)\right)\text{ where \ }\varphi(t,\xi)=\left(a-\mu\eta(t)\right)|\xi|,

(6.6)

where
\mu\geq\lambda
will be determined later, and
\eta(t)
is given by

\eta(t)=\int_{0}^{t}\left(\|(\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\Theta},\epsilon\partial_{x}u^{\epsilon}_{\Theta})(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)dt^{\prime}.

We can observe that, if we take
c_{0}
and
c_{1}
small enough in Theorems
1.1
and
1.2
then
\varphi(t)\geq 0
and

0\leq\varphi(t,\xi)\leq min\left(\phi(t,\xi),\Theta(t,\xi)\right).

Then in view of (
6.6
), we observe that
(G,H)_{\varphi}
verifies

\left\{\;\begin{aligned} &\partial_{t}G_{\varphi}+\mu\dot{\eta}(t)|D_{x}|G_{\varphi}+A_{\epsilon}(D)G_{\varphi}=\begin{pmatrix}0\\
F^{1}_{\varphi}+\partial_{x}q_{\varphi}\end{pmatrix}\\
&\epsilon^{2}\Big(\partial_{t}H_{\varphi}+\mu\dot{\eta}(t)|D_{x}|H_{\varphi}+B_{\epsilon}(D)H_{\varphi}\Big)=-\begin{pmatrix}0\\
F^{2}_{\varphi}+\partial_{y}q_{\varphi}\end{pmatrix}\\
&\partial_{x}R^{1}_{\varphi}+\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}=0\\
&(R^{1}_{\varphi},R^{2}_{\varphi})/_{y=0}=(R^{1}_{\varphi},R^{2}_{\varphi})/_{y=1}=0\end{aligned}\right.

(6.7)

Where
|D_{x}|
denote the Fourier multiplier of the symbol
|\xi|
. In what follows, we recall that we use ”C” to denote a generic positive constant which can change from line to line. Thanks to Theorems
1.1
and
1.2
, Propositions
4.1
and
4.2
, we deduce for
0<s<1
that

\|u^{\epsilon}_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+};\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\|(u+\partial_{t}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{+};\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}^{\frac{7}{2}})}+\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+};\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}^{\frac{7}{2}})}\\
+\|\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+};\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}\cap\mathcal{B}^{\frac{7}{2}})}+\|\partial_{t}^{2}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+};\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\leq M,

(6.8)

where
u^{\epsilon}_{\Theta}
and
u_{\phi}
are respectively determined by (
5.1
) and (
2.3
) and
M\geq 1
is a constant independent to
\epsilon
.

Proof of the theorem
1.3
We apply the dyadic operator in the horizontal variable
\Delta_{q}^{h}
to (
6.7
) and taking the
L^{2}
inner product of the resulting equation with
\Delta_{q}^{h}G_{\varphi}
and
\Delta_{q}^{h}H_{\varphi}
we obtain

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}G_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}G_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|G_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}G_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}A_{\epsilon}(D)G_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}G_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}q_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}

(6.9)

and

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{t}H_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}H_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|H_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}H_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}B_{\epsilon}(D)H_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}H_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
=-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}q_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

(6.10)

Due to the free divergence condition, we have

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\nabla q_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|=0.

Then by using Lemma
2.1
, we achieve

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\Big(\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\epsilon^{4}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\mu\dot{\eta}(t^{\prime})\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\mu\dot{\eta}(t^{\prime})\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\mu\dot{\eta}(t^{\prime})\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
+\mu\dot{\eta}(t^{\prime})\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\dot{\eta}(t^{\prime})\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{4}\mu\dot{\eta}(t^{\prime})\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
\leq\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi})\right\rangle\right|_{L^{2}}+\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle\right|_{L^{2}}.

(6.11)

Now we still have to take the inner product in
L^{2}
with
\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}
and
\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}
to the equation

\displaystyle e^{\varphi(t,|D_{x}|)}(\partial_{t}^{2}R^{1}+\partial_{t}R^{1}-\partial_{y}^{2}R^{1}-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}R^{1}+\partial_{x}p-F^{1})=0

\displaystyle e^{\varphi(t,|D_{x}|)}\Big(\epsilon^{2}(\partial_{t}^{2}R^{2}+\partial_{t}R^{2}-\partial_{y}^{2}R^{2}-\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}R^{2})+\partial_{y}p-F^{2}\Big)=0,

(6.12)

we obtain

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}^{2}R^{1}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}^{2}R^{1}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}q_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}},

(6.13)

and

\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}^{2}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{y}^{2}R^{2}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\\
-\epsilon^{2}\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon\partial_{x}^{2}R^{2}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}=\left\langle F^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}-\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}q_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}},

(6.14)

In what follows, we shall use again the technical lemmas in Section
2
, to handle term by term in the estimate (
6.13
) and (
6.14
). We start by the complicate term
I_{1}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}
and
I_{2}=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}^{2}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}
, so by using integration by parts, we find

\displaystyle I_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}dx-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}dx

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}dx-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}dx

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+2\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}dx

Then by using Lemma
2.1
, we achieve

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\Theta}dx-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\Theta}dx

\displaystyle+2\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\Theta}dx+\frac{d}{dt}\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\Theta}dx

\displaystyle-\int\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\Theta}dx+2\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\int\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\Theta}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\epsilon R^{2}_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\dot{\tau}(t)\|\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|^{\frac{1}{2}}(R^{1}_{\Theta},R^{2}_{\Theta})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{y}\epsilon R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}R^{1}_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}\partial_{x}\epsilon R^{2}_{\Theta}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(6.15)

\displaystyle=\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}+\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(F^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi})\right\rangle_{L^{2}}.

Now we claim that

\int_{0}^{t}\left|2\mu\dot{\eta}(t)\int\Big(\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\Theta}+\Delta_{q}^{h}|D_{x}|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\Theta}\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\Theta}\Big)dx\right|\\
\leq 2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\mu\Big(\|(\partial_{t}R^{1},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|\partial_{y}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\Theta}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\Big),

(6.16)

\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{1}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{1}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\\
\lesssim 2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\bigg(\epsilon\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}\Big(\|\epsilon R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\|\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\Big)\\
+\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\Big(\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\\
+\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\Big)+\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}\bigg),

(6.17)

and

\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{2}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{2}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\\
\lesssim 2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\Big\{\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|(R^{2}_{\varphi},(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B^{1}})}^{2}+\epsilon^{2}\|(R^{2}_{\varphi},\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\\
\times\Big(\|(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}+\|(\partial_{t}u)_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}+\epsilon\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{7}{2}})}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\Big)\\
+\epsilon^{2}\|(R^{2}_{\varphi},(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\Big(\|R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+\|u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\\
+\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})})\Big\},

(6.18)

the proof of those estimates will be presented later in the last section of the chapter.

By virtue of (
6.8
), (
6.17
) and (
6.18
), we infer

\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{i}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{i}_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{i}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{i})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\\
\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\Big(M\epsilon\|(\epsilon R^{1},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+M^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\\
+\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}+\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}+M\epsilon\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\\
+M^{\frac{3}{2}}\epsilon\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+M^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\epsilon\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\Big).

(6.19)

Then by summing
2\times\eqref{S9eq3bis1}
with
(
6
)
and we multiply the resulting by
2^{q}
and then integrating over time, and summing with respect to
q\in\mathbb{Z}
, we find that for
t<T^{\star}

\mathcal{K}(t)\leq\sqrt{C}\mathcal{E}(0)+\sqrt{C}\Big(\sqrt{M\epsilon}\|(\epsilon R^{1},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+M^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+\sqrt{M\epsilon}\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+M^{\frac{3}{4}}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+M^{\frac{1}{4}}\|\epsilon\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big),

(6.20)

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}(t)

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{1}{2}\|(R^{1}+\partial_{t}R^{1},\epsilon(R^{2}+\partial_{t}R^{2}))_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\frac{1}{2}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi},\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle+\|\partial_{y}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\epsilon\|\partial_{x}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{\infty}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\Big)+\|(\partial_{t}R^{1},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\mu}\|(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+\|\partial_{y}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\epsilon\|\partial_{x}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}_{t}^{2}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\mu}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+\sqrt{\mu}\|\partial_{y}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}+\sqrt{\mu}\|\partial_{x}(R^{1},\epsilon R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}(0)

\displaystyle=\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{y}(u^{\epsilon}_{0}-u_{0},\epsilon(v^{\epsilon}_{0}-v_{0}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\sqrt{C}\epsilon\|e^{a|D_{x}|}\partial_{x}(u^{\epsilon}_{0}-u_{0},\epsilon(v^{\epsilon}_{0}-v_{0}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{C}\|e^{a|D_{x}|}((u^{\epsilon}_{0}-u_{0})+(u^{\epsilon}_{1}-u_{1}),\epsilon(v^{\epsilon}_{0}-v_{0})+\epsilon(v^{\epsilon}_{1}-v_{1}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{C}\|e^{a|D_{x}|}((u^{\epsilon}_{1}-u_{1}),\epsilon(v^{\epsilon}_{1}-v_{1}))\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

Applying Young’s inequality gives rise to

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}(t)\leq\sqrt{C}\Big(\mathcal{E}(0)+M(\epsilon+\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}+\|(\epsilon R^{2},\epsilon\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}}(\mathcal{B}^{1})})\Big)

(6.21)

Taking
\mu=CM^{2}
lead to (
3.1
), this completes the proof of the theorem
1.3
.

7.
Proof of the estimates (
6.17
) and (
6.18
)

7.1.
Proof of the estimate (
6.17
)

We first observe that

\displaystyle F^{1}_{\varphi}=(\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u-(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{1}+R^{1}\partial_{x}u)-(v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}R^{1}+R^{2}\partial_{y}u))_{\varphi},

so, we define

\displaystyle G_{1}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}F^{1}_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u-(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{1}+R^{1}\partial_{x}u)-(v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}R^{1}+R^{2}\partial_{y}u))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\leq I_{1}^{q}+I_{2}^{q}+I_{3}^{q},

where

\displaystyle I_{1}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\epsilon^{2}\partial_{x}^{2}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{2}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{1}+R^{1}\partial_{x}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{3}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}R^{1}+R^{2}\partial_{y}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

We first observe that

\displaystyle I_{1}^{q}\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\epsilon^{2}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

(7.1)

For
I_{2}
, we write

\displaystyle I_{2}^{q}=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{1}+R^{1}\partial_{x}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\leq I_{21}+I_{22},

where

\displaystyle I_{21}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{22}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{1}\partial_{x}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

Lemma
2.5
implies

\displaystyle I_{21}^{q}\leq Cd_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})},

(7.2)

For
I_{22}
, using Bony’s decomposition for the horizontal variable, we write

R^{1}\partial_{x}u=T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}R^{1}+T^{h}_{R^{1}}\partial_{x}u+R^{h}(R^{1},\partial_{x}u),

and then, we have the following bound

\displaystyle I_{22}^{q}=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}R^{1}+T^{h}_{R^{1}}\partial_{x}u+R^{h}(R^{1},\partial_{x}u))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\leq I_{22,1}^{q}+I_{22,2}^{q}+I_{22,3}^{q}

with

\displaystyle I_{22,1}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{22,2}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{R^{1}}\partial_{x}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{22,3}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(R^{1},\partial_{x}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

Using the support properties given in [
[
5
]
, Proposition 2.10] and the definition of
T^{h}_{R^{1}}\partial_{x}u
, we have

\displaystyle I_{22,2}^{q}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}.

As we have
R^{1}=u^{\epsilon}-u
, so

\displaystyle\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}=\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}-u)_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}},

then,

\displaystyle I_{22,2}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}.

We note that we get by applying Bernstein lemma
2.1

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q^{\prime}}(u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi})\|u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

Here and in all that follows, we always denote
(d_{q^{\prime}}(u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}))_{q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}}
to be a generic element of
\ell^{1}(\mathbb{Z})
so that
\sum_{q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}}d_{q^{\prime}}(u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi})\leq 1
, then

\displaystyle\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{ \ and \ }\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

So

\displaystyle I_{22,2}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\|u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\big(\|u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

Now, we recall that

\|S_{q-1}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\sum_{l\leq q-2}2^{\frac{3l}{2}}\|\Delta_{l}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{l}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 2^{q}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}},

so we can deduce

\displaystyle I_{22,1}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle\right|\leq\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}2^{q^{\prime}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{{}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

Using the definition of
\dot{\eta}(t)
and Definition
2.3
we have

\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 2^{-q}d_{q}(R^{1}_{\varphi})\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

Then,

I_{22,1}^{q}\lesssim 2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}

where

d_{q}^{2}=d_{q}\left(\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}d_{q^{\prime}}\right)

In a similar way, we have

\displaystyle I_{22,3}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(R^{1},\partial_{x}u))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\tilde{\Delta}_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\int_{0}^{t}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}\right)\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}

Then we conclude that

\displaystyle I_{22}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\Big(\|(R^{1},\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}+\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{2}\Big)

(7.3)

For the term
I_{3}^{q}
, we write

\displaystyle I_{3}^{q}=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}R^{1}+R^{2}\partial_{y}u))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\leq I_{31}^{q}+I_{32}^{q},

(7.4)

where

\displaystyle I_{31}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{32}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{2}\partial_{y}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

Since

v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}R^{1}=(R^{2}+v)\partial_{y}R^{1}=R^{2}\partial_{y}R^{1}+v\partial_{y}R^{1},

we get

\displaystyle I_{31}^{q}\leq I_{31,1}^{q}+I_{31,2}^{q},

with

\displaystyle I_{31,1}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{2}\partial_{y}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{31,2}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

Lemma
2.6
implies

\displaystyle I_{31,1}^{q}=\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

(7.5)

For the term
I_{31,2}^{q}
, we apply Bony’s decomposition with respect to the horizontal variable

v\partial_{y}R^{1}=T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}R^{1}+T^{h}_{\partial_{y}R^{1}}v+R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}R^{1}).

Using (
3.1
), we have

\displaystyle\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}=\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}(t,x,s)ds\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{l\leq q^{\prime}-2}2^{\frac{3l}{2}}\|\Delta_{l}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{l}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

from which, we infer

\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}R^{1})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\\
\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}\\
\leq\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}\\
\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})},

(7.6)

where
\left\{d_{q}\right\}
forms a suitable sequence.

As we have
R^{1}=u^{\epsilon}-u
, so

\displaystyle\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}=\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}(u^{\epsilon}-u)_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}},

then,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}R^{1}}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime},

We note that we get by applying Bernstein lemma
2.1

\displaystyle\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\lesssim\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\text{ \ and \ }\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\lesssim\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

So,

\displaystyle I_{22,2}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v}))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v}))}^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|q-q^{\prime}|\leq 4}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{t}(L^{2})}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\big(\|\partial_{y}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

where
\left\{d_{q}\right\}
forms a suitable sequence.

Finally, by using that

\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}(t,x,y)\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\lesssim d_{q^{\prime}}(u_{\phi})\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}R^{1}))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\tilde{\Delta}_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\tilde{\Delta}_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})},

Then we obtaine the following estimates,

\displaystyle I_{31,2}^{q}\lesssim 2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

(7.7)

Now we estimate the term
I_{32}^{q}
in (
7.4
). Bony’s decomposition with respect to the horizontal variable implies

\displaystyle I_{32}^{q}=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T_{R^{2}}^{h}\partial_{y}u+T^{h}_{\partial_{y}u}R^{2}+R^{h}(R^{2},\partial_{y}u))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\leq I_{32,1}^{q}+I_{32,2}^{q}+I_{32,3}^{q},

where

\displaystyle I_{32,1}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T_{R^{2}}^{h}\partial_{y}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{32,2}^{q}
