\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}u}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle I_{32,3}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(R^{2},\partial_{y}u))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

We first observe that

\displaystyle I_{32,1}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}2^{\frac{-q^{\prime}}{2}}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}.

Due to the fact that
R^{2}(t,x,y)=-\int_{0}^{y}\partial_{x}R^{1}(t,x,s)ds
, we deduce

\displaystyle I_{32,1}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}2^{\frac{-q^{\prime}}{2}}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\int_{0}^{y}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{x}R^{1}_{\varphi}(t,x,s)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dsdt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

Taking into account the definition of
\dot{\eta}(t)
and Definition
2.3
we obtain

\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim 2^{-\frac{q}{2}}d_{q}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

Then,

\displaystyle I_{32,1}^{q}\lesssim 2^{-q}d_{q}^{2}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}

(7.8)

Now, for
I_{32,2}^{q}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{32,2}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}2^{q^{\prime}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

We end by estimate
I_{32,3}^{q}
, in the same way, we have

\displaystyle I_{32,3}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\int_{0}^{t}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\|\tilde{\Delta}_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\int_{0}^{t}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q}{2}}\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})},

Summing all the resulting estimate, we can achieve

\displaystyle I_{32}^{q}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{1}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|^{\prime}\partial_{t}R^{1})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

(7.9)

Remark 7.1
.

For the proof when we have
R^{1}
and not
\partial_{t}R^{1}
, it also the some we need just to replace in all the proof with
R^{1}
instead of
\partial_{t}R^{1}
.

By summing up (
7.1
)-(
7.9
), we conclude the proof of (
6.17
).

8.
Proof of lemma
4.1

In this appendix we present the proof of the lemma
4.1
, we start first by giving the proof of the estimate (
4.25
). By using the Bony’s decomposition (
2.2
), we can write

u\partial_{x}u=T^{h}_{u}\partial_{x}u+T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}u+R^{h}(u,\partial_{x}u).

Then

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{u}\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(u,\partial_{x}u))_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

We use the definition of
T^{h}
, we have

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{u}\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(S^{h}_{q^{\prime}-1}u\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The lemma
2.2
allows us to obtain

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(S^{h}_{q^{\prime}-1}u\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{+}_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

(8.1)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{+}_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}^{2}2^{2q^{\prime}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

(8.2)

Using Bernstein Lemma
2.1
we have

\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}}\lesssim 2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}}.

So we can deduce that

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q^{\prime}}(u_{\phi})\|u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}

Here and in all that follows, we always denote
(d_{q}(u_{\phi}))_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}
to be a generic element of
\ell^{1}(\mathbb{Z})
so that
\sum\limits_{q\in\mathbb{Z}}d_{q}(u_{\phi})\leq 1
.

Then,

\displaystyle\|S^{h}_{q^{\prime}-1}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}\lesssim\|u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}.

While using the inclusion
H^{1}_{y}\hookrightarrow L^{\infty}_{y}
,

\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{v}}\lesssim\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{v}}^{\frac{1}{2}},

and the Poincaré inequality on the interval
\{0<y<1\}
on
u
(as we have that
u=0
when y = 0,1)

\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}},

we obtain

\displaystyle\|\Delta^{h}_{q^{\prime}}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}.

(8.3)

We replace in (
8.1
) we obtain

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{u}\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}2^{q^{\prime}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}2^{q^{\prime}}\Big(2^{-\frac{5q^{\prime}}{2}}d_{q^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}u_{\phi})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}2^{-\frac{3q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})},

(8.4)

where

d_{q}=\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}d_{q^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}u_{\phi})2^{\frac{3}{2}(q-q^{\prime})}.

Along the same way we can obtain that

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}u}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq Cd_{q}2^{-\frac{3q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}.

We still have to find the estimate of the rest term, we have

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(u,\partial_{x}u))_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{D}_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}_{h}(L^{2}_{v})}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}2^{2q^{\prime}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

By using the Poincaré inequality we achieve

\displaystyle\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})}\lesssim\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}.

Then

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(u,\partial_{x}u))_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}2^{2q^{\prime}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}2^{-\frac{3q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}.

By summing all the resulting estimates, and then multiplying by
2^{\frac{3q}{2}}
and taking the sum over
\mathbb{Z}
we obtain the proof of the term
\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(u\partial_{x}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}.

Now we give the proof of the second estimate in (
4.25
). By using the Bony’s decomposition (
2.2
), we can write

v\partial_{y}u=T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}u+T^{h}_{\partial_{y}u}v+R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}u).

Then

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}u}v)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}u))_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

We use the definition of
T^{h}
, we have

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(S^{h}_{q^{\prime}-1}v\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

The lemma
2.2
allows us to obtain

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}(S^{h}_{q^{\prime}-1}v\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}v^{+}_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}_{h}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(8.5)

We have by applying Bernstein Lemma
2.1
that

\displaystyle\|\Delta^{h}_{q^{\prime}}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{\frac{3q}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\int_{0}^{y}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime},.,s)ds\|_{L^{2}_{h}(L^{\infty}_{v})},

we use the fact that
\|\int_{0}^{y}fdy^{\prime}\|_{L^{\infty}_{y}}\leq\int_{0}^{1}|f|dy^{\prime}\leq\|f\|_{L^{2}_{y}}
. As a result, it comes out

\displaystyle\|\Delta^{h}_{q^{\prime}}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{\frac{3q}{2}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q^{\prime}}(u_{\phi})2^{q^{\prime}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}},

where
(d_{q^{\prime}}(u_{\phi}))_{q^{\prime}\in\mathbb{Z}}
is a generic element of
\ell^{1}(\mathbb{Z})
such that
\sum d_{q^{\prime}}(u_{\phi})\leq 1
. Then

\displaystyle\|S^{h}_{q^{\prime}-1}v_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 2^{q^{\prime}}\|u_{\phi}(t^{\prime})\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}},

We replace in (
8.5
) we obtain

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{v}\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}2^{q^{\prime}}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}2^{q^{\prime}}\Big(2^{-\frac{5q^{\prime}}{2}}d_{q^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}u_{\phi})\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}2^{-\frac{3q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})},

(8.6)

where

d_{q}=\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}d_{q^{\prime}}(\partial_{y}u_{\phi})2^{\frac{3}{2}(q-q^{\prime})}.

Along the same way we can obtain that

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{y}u}v)_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq Cd_{q}2^{-\frac{3q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}.

We finish our proof by giving the estimate of the last term
\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}u))_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}
. We use the definition of
R^{h}
, we have

\displaystyle\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(v,\partial_{y}u))_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{\Delta}_{q^{\prime}}^{h}v_{\phi}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{q^{\prime}\geq q-3}\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}}}^{2}2^{-2q^{\prime}}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t^{\prime}}\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd_{q}2^{-\frac{3q}{2}}\|u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{5}{2}})}\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}\partial_{y}u_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

By summing all the resulting estimates, and then multiplying by
2^{\frac{3q}{2}}
and taking the sum over
\mathbb{Z}
we obtain the proof of the term
\|e^{\mathcal{R}t}(v\partial_{y}u)_{\phi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}.

8.1.
Proof of the estimate (
6.18
)

We first deduce from
\partial_{x}u+\partial_{y}v=0
, that

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\epsilon^{2}\|(\partial_{t}u)_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}^{2}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{q}\epsilon^{2}\|(\partial_{t}^{2}u)_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})},

\displaystyle\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{y}^{2}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\epsilon^{2}\|\partial_{y}^{2}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})},

\displaystyle\epsilon^{4}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{x}^{2}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\epsilon^{4}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{7}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}.

(8.7)

Then now we still have to control

\displaystyle J_{4}^{q}=\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}v^{\epsilon})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

and

\displaystyle J_{5}^{q}=\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

We start first by
J^{4}_{q}
, we have

\displaystyle J_{4}^{q}\leq\epsilon^{2}\left(J_{41}^{q}+J_{42}^{q}\right),

where

\displaystyle J_{41}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle J_{42}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

It follows from Lemma
2.5
that

\displaystyle J_{41}^{q}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

(8.8)

For the second term, Bony’s decomposition for the horizontal variable gives

\displaystyle J_{42}^{q}=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(u^{\epsilon}\partial_{x}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\leq J_{421}^{q}+J_{422}^{q}+J_{423}^{q},

with

\displaystyle J_{421}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle J_{422}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}v}u^{\epsilon})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle J_{423}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(u^{\epsilon},\partial_{x}v))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}.

Due to

\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\|u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}},

and the relation (
3.1
), we have

\displaystyle J_{421}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{u^{\epsilon}}\partial_{x}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}u^{\epsilon}_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}\partial_{x}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|q^{\prime}-q|\leq 4}\int_{0}^{t}\|u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}}}2^{2q^{\prime}}\|\Delta_{q^{\prime}}^{h}u_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{2})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

While again thanks to (
3.1
), we find

\displaystyle\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{x}v_{\varphi}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\int_{0}^{y}\|S_{q^{\prime}-1}^{h}\partial_{x}(\partial_{x}u_{\varphi}(t,x,s)\|_{L^{\infty}}ds\lesssim 2^{\frac{q^{\prime}}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\mathcal{B}^{2}},

which leads to

\displaystyle J_{422}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(T^{h}_{\partial_{x}v}u^{\epsilon})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{2})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

Along the same way, we obtain

\displaystyle J_{423}^{q}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{h}(\partial_{x}v,u^{\epsilon}))_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{2})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

This gives rise to

\displaystyle J_{42}^{q}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}^{\epsilon}\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{2})}\|\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

(8.9)

Now for
J_{5}^{q}
, We first note that

v^{\epsilon}\partial_{y}v^{\epsilon}=v\partial_{y}R^{2}+R^{2}\partial_{y}R^{2}+v\partial_{y}v+R^{2}\partial_{y}v.

Lemma
2.5
yields

\epsilon^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{2}\partial_{y}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|(R^{1}_{\varphi},\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi})\|^{2}_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

From
(
7.7
)
, we have

\int_{0}^{t}\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}R^{2})_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

As for
(
7.3
)
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(R^{2}\partial_{x}u)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}.\hskip 56.9055pt

Then, we deduce from the proof of
(
7.7
)
that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\Delta_{q}^{h}(v\partial_{y}v)_{\varphi},\Delta_{q}^{h}(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\right\rangle_{L^{2}}\right|dt^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}v_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}.

As a result, it comes out

\displaystyle J_{5}^{q}

\displaystyle\lesssim d_{q}^{2}2^{-q}\Big(\|(R^{1}_{\varphi},\epsilon(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi})\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}^{2}

\displaystyle+\epsilon^{2}\|u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{\infty}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|\partial_{y}R^{2}_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{1}{2}})}+\|\partial_{y}u_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t}(\mathcal{B}^{\frac{3}{2}})})\|(\partial_{t}R^{2})_{\varphi}\|_{\tilde{L}^{2}_{t,\dot{\eta}(t)}(\mathcal{B}^{1})}\Big).

(8.10)

Remark 8.1
.

For the proof when we have
R^{2}
and not
\partial_{t}R^{2}
, it also the same we need just to replace in all the proof with
R^{2}
instead of
\partial_{t}R^{2}
.

By summing up (
8.1
)-(
8.1
), we conclude the proof of (
6.18
)
